Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The immunology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 801 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 692 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to immunology theory, we decompose the problem into 801 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 692. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
